# Skills · H · part 34

867 entries, hic-contact-map-downsampling-and-realignment to hig-getting-started-designing-for-watchos. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **hic-contact-map-downsampling-and-realignment**: Use when a deeply sequenced Hi-C dataset must be reduced to controlled sequencing-depth levels to test how data depth affects a structure-prediction or interaction-detection model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-map-downsampling-and-realignment))
- **hic-contact-map-feature-annotation**: Use when you have completed Hi-C map generation (producing .hic files from aligned reads) and need to detect and annotate topological features such as chromatin loops, topologically associating domains (TADs), or interaction peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/hic-contact-map-feature-annotation))
- **hic-contact-map-loading-and-normalization**: Use when raw Hi-C contact maps (.cool files or Juicer dump text) must be loaded, KR-normalized, converted to O/E matrices, and sequencedepth-aligned before patch sampling or loop calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-map-loading-and-normalization))
- **hic-contact-map-loop-prediction**: Use when you have a .cool/.hic genome-wide contact map at ~10 kb resolution plus a bedpe positive training set from an orthogonal assay (ChIA-PET, HiChIP, Capture Hi-C) and need supervised chromatin loop predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-map-loop-prediction))
- **hic-contact-map-percentile-normalization**: Use when raw Hi-C contact frequency matrices (e.g. KR-normalized 5 kb bins) must be converted into distance-aware, percentile-binned skeleton labels to train or run inference with deepC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-map-percentile-normalization))
- **hic-contact-map-preprocessing**: Use when raw Hi-C matrices from new cell types must be reformatted into the DNALONGBENCH/Akita 448x448 contact map .tfr format so that load_data(task_name='contact_map_prediction') can consume them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-map-preprocessing))
- **hic-contact-map-reproducibility-scoring**: Use when you have two 40 kb-resolution Hi-C contact maps (e.g., biological replicates, normalized vs reference, or downsampled vs full data) and need a single reproducibility score to compare normalization methods or sequencing-depth robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-map-reproducibility-scoring))
- **hic-contact-map-target-processing**: Use when raw Hi-C contact matrices must be converted into fixed-resolution (5 kb skeleton) binned interaction targets to train a sequence-based chromatin interaction predictor such as deepC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-map-target-processing))
- **hic-contact-matrix-construction**: Use when you have a PCR-duplicate-removed ChIA-PET bedpe file (e.g. prefixname.rmdup.bedpe) and need a genome-binned 2D contact map for visualization instead of, or in addition to, loop calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-matrix-construction))
- **hic-contact-matrix-downsampling**: Use when benchmarking loop callers across sequencing depths, training GILoop branches at their preferred matrix density (GCN at 10%, U-Net at full depth), or testing robustness of CTCF loop calling on sparse Hi-C libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-matrix-downsampling))
- **hic-contact-matrix-graph-modeling**: Use when raw (non-imputed) bulk or single-cell Hi-C contact matrices must be converted into weighted-graph inputs for structural-entropy-based TLD/TAD prediction, including sparse single-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-matrix-graph-modeling))
- **hic-contact-matrix-noise-perturbation**: Use when benchmarking TAD/TLD predictors for robustness on sparse Hi-C data, e.g., single-cell-level downsampled bulk Hi-C matrices (0.025% sampling rate, 40 kb binsize), by comparing predictions on noisy vs. original matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-matrix-noise-perturbation))
- **hic-contact-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw .hic files (e.g., GM12878 accession 4DNFI1UEG1HD from 4DN/GSE63525) must be dumped into NxN KR-normalized matrices and chunked into 400x400 submatrices before GAE-based TAD calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-matrix-preprocessing))
- **hic-contact-matrix-tld-detection**: Use when input is a raw intra-chromosome Hi-C contact matrix (n×n or sparse edge format) from bulk or single-cell data and hierarchical TAD/TLD boundaries must be predicted, especially for sparse scHi-C where imputation is undesirable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-matrix-tld-detection))
- **hic-contact-normalization**: Use when raw Hi-C or capture Hi-C fragment-pair tables (frag1, frag2, cov_frag1, cov_frag2, freq, dist) need bias removal, distance-background normalization, or significance calling of cis long-range contacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hic-contact-normalization))
- **hic-contact-scaffolding**: Use when draft contigs (original or split at CSE misjoin breakpoints) must be assembled into chromosome-scale scaffolds with Hi-C contact maps, and when comparing scaffold quality before vs. after chimeric-error correction via N50, R-AQI, and S-AQI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/hic-contact-scaffolding))

- **hic-matrix-qc**: This skill performs standardized quality control (QC) on Hi-C contact matrices stored in .mcool or .cool format. It computes coverage and cis/trans ratios, distance-dependent contact decay (P(s) curves), coverage uniformity, and replicate correlation at a chosen resolution using cooler and cooltools. Use it to assess whether Hi-C data are of sufficient quality for downstream analyses such as TAD calling, loop detection, and compartment analysis. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/tools/hic-matrix-qc-majiayu000) · ★67 · 6 copies)
- **hic-matrix-resolution-to-bin-length-conversion**: Use when you have per-bin cluster labels from an N×N intra-chromosomal Hi-C contact matrix and need to delineate TAD boundaries as genomic intervals for downstream visualization or evaluation. - **hic-matrix-visualization-compatibility**: Use when a ChIA-PET bedpe interaction file (e.g. in.rmDup.bedpe) needs to be binned into a genome-wide 2D contact matrix for heatmap-style visualization with Hi-C tools such as HiCPlotter. Also activate on Spanish triggers: "montá un sistema de agentes", "bootstrapeá agentes", "armá la jerarquía", "iniciá… ([rodrigosiboldi/hierarchical-agent-system](https://github.com/rodrigosiboldi/hierarchical-agent-system/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **hierarchical-agents**: Generate hierarchical AGENTS.md structure for codebases to optimize AI agent token usage. Use when creating AGENTS.md files, documenting codebase structure, setting up agent guidance, organizing project documentation for AI tools, implementing JIT indexing, or working with monorepos that need lightweight root guidance with detailed sub-folder documentation. Covers repository analysis, root AGENTS.md generation, sub-folder AGENTS.md creation, and token-efficient documentation patterns. ([majesticlabs-dev/majestic-marketplace](https://github.com/majesticlabs-dev/majestic-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/majestic-engineer/skills/hierarchical-agents) · ★50)
- **hierarchical-agents-md**: 层级 AGENTS.md / README.md：init、update、audit、reorganize、trim。 初始化 Agent 指令、代码改动后同步文档、检查文档漂移、重组仓库文档层级、精简规则与 Skills。 Not for 写业务代码或生成文档站点。 ([rockcookies/skills](https://github.com/rockcookies/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/custom/hierarchical-agents-md) · ★1)
- **hierarchical-and-matrix-visualization**: Hierarchical, matrix, and composition visualization skill for calendar charts, Gantt charts, heatmaps, stem-and-leaf plots, tally charts, timetables, circle packing, donut charts, dot matrix charts, Nightingale rose charts, parallel sets, pictogram charts, pie charts, proportional area charts, sunburst diagrams, treemaps, and word clouds. ([smallbearrr/data2graph-](https://github.com/smallbearrr/data2graph-/tree/HEAD/app/services/crewai_flow/skills/visualization-skills/hierarchical-and-matrix-visualization))
- **hierarchical-attention-neuromarketing-prediction**: Applies Hierarchical Attention-based Deep Neural Networks (HA-DNN) to fuse EEG neural biomarkers with social-media sentiment for predicting individual purchasing decisions, achieving 86.7% accuracy and AUC 0.91. Use when integrating neural and social signals for consumer behavior prediction, building multimodal neuromarketing models, or evaluating whether social sentiment adds value beyond neural signals alone. NOT for single-modality neuromarketing (EEG-only or sentiment-only) or for real-time inference where latency constraints preclude deep models. ([hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library](https://github.com/hamishfromatech/my-personal-skills-library/tree/HEAD/marketing-and-content/hierarchical-attention-neuromarketing-prediction))
- **hierarchical-average-linkage-clustering**: Use when you have a plant-species (or site) x arthropod-OTU count table from eDNA metabarcoding and need to visualise/quantify whether community composition clusters by sample grouping via average-linkage dendrograms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/hierarchical-average-linkage-clustering))
- **hierarchical-average-linkage-clustering**: Use when m ≥ 3 lipidomes have been placed in a common structural space and you need a global lipidome-level relationship model (dendrogram) from their pairwise Hausdorff distances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hierarchical-average-linkage-clustering))
- **hierarchical-batch-integration**: Use when integrating atlas-scale scRNA-seq collections (many studies, thousands of samples, millions of cells) where rapid methods like Harmony or scVI return only embeddings, and an adjusted expression matrix plus preserved biological condition signal is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-batch-integration))
- **hierarchical-batch-merge-orchestration**: Use when a large-scale metabolomics study spans multiple acquisition batches (e.g. 96-well plates across many days) with inter-batch replicate pairs embedded, and between-batch unwanted variation must be removed via RUV-III in a hierarchical merge design. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hierarchical-batch-merge-orchestration))
- **hierarchical-batch-merging-structure-comparison**: Use when hRUV-normalising a large-scale metabolomics cohort (e.g. BioHEART-CT plasma batches) and you must justify the choice of negative-control metabolites or the hierarchical merging design rather than accepting defaults. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hierarchical-batch-merging-structure-comparison))
- **hierarchical-bayesian-count-modeling**: Use when raw per-sample cell-cluster count vectors n_lk (from an AnnData of single-cell samples) must be converted into sample-level probability distributions over clusters before optimal-transport or distance computations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-bayesian-count-modeling))
- **hierarchical-bayesian-em-mcmc-fitting**: Use when gene-level DE summary statistics (beta, beta_var) plus gene set annotations are available and you need calibrated per-gene-set enrichment p-values that borrow information across genes rather than analyzing one gene at a time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-bayesian-em-mcmc-fitting))
- **hierarchical-bayesian-joint-de-gse-modeling**: Use when you have gene-level summary statistics (beta coefficients and coefficient variances) from a scRNA-seq DE method and want joint DE + GSE analysis with calibrated gene set p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-bayesian-joint-de-gse-modeling))
- **hierarchical-bayesian-statistical-learning-eeg**: Hierarchical Bayesian Statistical Learning (HBSL) model for individual statistical learning trajectories from EEG data. Models how individuals discover structure in sensory sequences, with applications to dyslexia research and cognitive development. Activation: hierarchical Bayesian, statistical learning, EEG, individual differences, dyslexia, sequence structure, tone sequences. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/reasoning-bayesian/hierarchical-bayesian-statistical-learning-eeg) · ★4)
- **hierarchical-brain-criticality**: Hierarchical organization of critical brain dynamics. Studies how criticality signatures vary along anatomical hierarchy in brain systems using phenomenological renormalization group approaches on large-scale neuronal spiking data. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/hierarchical-brain-criticality) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **hierarchical-bsq-codebook-quantization**: Quantize continuous vectors into hierarchical discrete tokens via Binary Spherical Quantization with a high-bit "pre" head and a low-bit "post" head. ([kjuhwa/skills-hub](https://github.com/kjuhwa/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/ml-ops/hierarchical-bsq-codebook-quantization))
- **hierarchical-bubble-tree-organization**: Use when a colored de Bruijn graph (directed, acyclic, nondegenerate, unique start/end nodes, node color sets per sample) must be decomposed into colored superbubbles and arranged into a parent-child tree that supports tri-tuple coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hierarchical-bubble-tree-organization))
- **hierarchical-cell-ontology-label-aggregation**: Use when comparing cell-label predictors (e.g. zero-shot scPRINT vs CellTypist vs supervised baselines) on labels whose granularity differs across methods or annotation schemes, and you need ontology-consistent, coarser-grained evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cell-ontology-label-aggregation))
- **hierarchical-cell-type-annotation-scoring**: Use when a gene-by-cell scRNA-seq count matrix from a tumour biopsy must be annotated into cancer, immune, and stromal cell types/subtypes across a pan-cancer hierarchy with intermediate per-class confidence scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cell-type-annotation-scoring))
- **hierarchical-cell-type-classification**: Use when annotating scRNA-seq data (Seurat v4 objects with a UMAP reduction) at the cell-type level, or when identifying malignant cells and predicting their likely cell of origin in normal or cancer tissues. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cell-type-classification--a93b40e228893d74))
- **hierarchical-cell-type-classification**: Use when annotating human single-cell or bulk RNA-seq cells against the Cell Ontology, especially when flat classifiers would be inconsistent with the ontology hierarchy or when cells should be placed at an internal (ancestral) ontology node rather than a leaf. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cell-type-classification--8831870ab9ce050b))
- **hierarchical-cell-type-marker-selection**: Use when scRNA-seq data comes with a multi-level cell-type label hierarchy and the goal is a minimal marker panel that discriminates all levels jointly (e.g. selecting 30–40 markers for the Zeisel mouse cortex labels). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cell-type-marker-selection))
- **hierarchical-cell-type-model-classification**: Use when bulk DIE genes (from an Iso-based differential isoform test) must be attributed to constituent cell types — deciding whether a region-specific isoform shift originates in one cell type, multiple cell types, or no cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cell-type-model-classification))
- **hierarchical-centroid-distance-linkage**: Decision support for clustering a wide-formatted metabolomics feature table (samples as columns, metabolomics features as rows) to visualize group structure via a clustered heatmap with dendrograms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hierarchical-centroid-distance-linkage))
- **hierarchical-chromosome-cluster-functional-annotation**: Use when you have a BHi-Cect nested cluster list plus ChIP-seq tracks and want to know which GO biological functions are enriched among genes whose loci fall into specific bottom enclaves. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-chromosome-cluster-functional-annotation))
- **hierarchical-chunking**: Chunk nested documents into parent-child levels so retrieval can move from broad sections to fine-grained passages. ([Goodnight77/rag-skills](https://github.com/Goodnight77/rag-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chunking/hierarchical-chunking) · ★8)
- **hierarchical-classification**: Comprehensive guide to hierarchical classification. Master the concepts, implementation, best practices, and real-world applications of hierarchical classification in professional environments. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/hierarchical-classification) · ★1)
- **hierarchical-classifier-configuration**: Use when annotating human scRNA-seq cells against the Cell Ontology with a pre-trained, ready-to-run classifier, or when benchmarking CellO against scMatch/SingleR by projecting flat labels onto ontology term IDs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-classifier-configuration))
- **hierarchical-classifier-rejection-step**: Use when a labeled single-cell training set and an unlabeled test set must be screened for cells that do not belong to any known population, i.e., rejection of potential unseen cell populations before hierarchical classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-classifier-rejection-step))
- **hierarchical-classifier-training**: Use when an annotated scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., simulated AMB data or PBMC-FACS/AMB2018 from the scRNA-seq benchmark Zenodo) must be classified through a hierarchical tree and cells from held-out or unknown populations must be flagged as 'unseen' rather than misassigned. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-classifier-training))
- **hierarchical-cluster-analysis-dendrogram-generation**: Use when you have a feature-by-sample matrix (finalData) of metabolomic intensities and sample group labels (finalLabel), and you want to visualize how samples cluster together based on their overall metabolomic similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cluster-analysis-dendrogram-generation))
- **hierarchical-cluster-structure-generation**: Use when benchmarking transfer-learning clustering on scRNA-Seq data requires synthetic count matrices with known ground-truth labels and controlled complete/incomplete/no-overlap between source and target cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cluster-structure-generation))
- **hierarchical-cluster-then-annotate-cell-classification**: Use when annotating tumor-microenvironment cell subtypes in cancer scRNA-seq data and direct per-cell subtype prediction is inaccurate; apply after major-type classification to boost subtype accuracy on T/NK or myeloid cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-cluster-then-annotate-cell-classification))
- **hierarchical-clustering**: Comprehensive guide to hierarchical clustering. Master the concepts, implementation, best practices, and real-world applications of hierarchical clustering in professional environments. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/hierarchical-clustering) · ★1)
- **hierarchical-clustering-correlation-distance**: Use when you need to reveal hidden multi-level structure among variables (or objects) in a high-dimensional galaxy-catalog or photometric table without pre-specifying the number of clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-correlation-distance))
- **hierarchical-clustering-cut-selection**: Use when a dendrogram from AMADA's hierarchical clustering must be cut into a partition, and no prior number of clusters is specified — the Calinski-Harabasz index guides the cut height choice. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-cut-selection))
- **hierarchical-clustering-cutree-cutoff-application**: Use when a batch-corrected (Supervised Harmony) embedding matrix with one row per cell is available and you need a single flat cluster assignment per cell to feed get_clusters() downstream in SingCellaR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-cutree-cutoff-application))
- **hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-construction**: Use when you have a full pairwise similarity-index matrix (Ssim, Psim, PsimW, Csim) for a set of organisms and want to test whether index-based groupings reproduce known evolutionary or phylogenetic structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-construction))
- **hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-construction**: Use when when you have a Metaboprep object after quality control filtering and need to visualize metabolite correlation structure to: (1) identify clusters of co-regulated features that may represent the same biological pathway or measurement artifact, (2) set tree-cutting height thresholds for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-construction))
- **hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-cutting**: Use when after XCMS feature detection, grouping, retention time correction, and missing value filling have produced an aligned feature matrix, when you need to group features (m/z, retention time pairs) that likely originate from the same metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-cutting) · 2 copies)
- **hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-generation**: Use when you have a normalized and imputed metabolite abundance matrix (as a MultiAssayExperiment object) and want to identify groups of co-expressed metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-generation))
- **hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-interpretation**: Use when you have a pre-computed hierarchical dendrogram from correlation-based clustering of LC-MS features (with fixed linkage criterion and distance metric) and need to decide whether a single constant-threshold cut or data-driven silhouette optimization better resolves the underlying cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-interpretation) · 3 copies)
- **hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-interpretation**: Use when normalized microarray intensity samples need QC for systematic batch effects: the analysis goal is to see whether samples group by date, operator, or reagent lot rather than by response class before downstream testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-interpretation))
- **hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-plotting**: Use when you need a tree representation of hidden structure among variables of a high-dimensional astronomical dataset (e.g. SDSS supernova hosts, Millennium mock catalogs, ZENS groups) and an optimal cluster count without pre-specifying it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-dendrogram-plotting))
- **hierarchical-clustering-euclidean-complete-linkage**: Use when you have a normalized microbe-metabolite feature attribution score matrix (rows = microbes, columns = metabolites) and need to explore hierarchical structure and visually assess similarity patterns before determining the optimal number of clusters via consensus clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-euclidean-complete-linkage))
- **hierarchical-clustering-heatmap-visualization**: Use when you have per-tissue significant sQTL tables (sqtls-*fdr.nominal.tsv) indexed by geneId-snpId variant-gene pairs and need to test whether sQTL effect sizes are shared across tissues and whether tissue groups (e.g. brain vs non-brain) cluster apart. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-heatmap-visualization))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-cell-types**: Use when deconvolving bulk RNA-seq cell type proportions with MuSiC and the scRNA-seq reference contains closely related cell types (e.g., PT vs DCT, CD-PC vs CD-IC) where collinearity destabilizes single-stage W-NNLS estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-cell-types))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-correlation-values**: Use when you have log-e-normalized expression of candidate uTARs plus canonical cell-type marker genes (e.g., HBZ, TNNT2, COL1A1) over matching spots/cells and need to test which uTARs are cell-type specific or spatially colocalized, and to visualize groupings as a dendrogram. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-correlation-values))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-epigenomic-profiles**: Use when a sparse single-cell count matrix (scChIP-seq/scATAC-seq bins or peaks) with known or hypothesized sub-populations must be grouped via hierarchical clustering on a PCA/feature-space embedding and quantitatively benchmarked by ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-epigenomic-profiles))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-expression-matrix**: Use when input is a gene expression matrix CSV (e.g. heatmap_test.csv) plus a sample annotation CSV (e.g. group_info.csv) and the goal is a clustered, group-annotated heatmap figure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-expression-matrix))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-marker-genes**: Use when generating an in-session CellexalVR heatmap after a user defines two or more cell groups, and the top-ranked marker genes and cells need to be ordered by hierarchical clustering for visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-marker-genes))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-metagenome-samples**: Use when you have raw 16S rRNA or WGS metagenome reads for a sample set and need to cluster samples by community composition and abundance, or to compare how different distance metrics (Libra cosine, Mash MinHash, Simka) resolve sample groupings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-metagenome-samples))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-msms-spectra**: Use when a set of curated MS/MS spectra (e.g., NIST 17 or MoNA entries) carries instrument type and collision-energy metadata and the goal is to determine whether spectral similarity is driven by acquisition conditions (HCD, QqQ, Q-TOF, IT) rather than by the underlying compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-msms-spectra))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-pwm-motifs**: Use when a large set of predicted sequence motifs (e.g. DESSO motifs from 690 ENCODE ChIP-seq datasets) must be de-duplicated into representative motifs and classified as matching documented JASPAR/TRANSFAC motifs or as novel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-pwm-motifs))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-sample-decay-rates**: Use when a base-level RNA-seq cohort shows 3'-to-5' coverage decline in some samples and you must identify and exclude degraded samples so they do not appear as spurious shape-change outliers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-sample-decay-rates))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-single-cell-transcriptomes**: Use when you must group individual cells from a single-cell expression matrix into an initial set of discrete cell-state clusters prior to defining Mpath landmark clusters, using FACS-sort labels to judge cluster quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-single-cell-transcriptomes))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-single-cells**: Use when you already have SClineager MCMC output (results.RData with inferred `genotype_mat` and `sigma`) and need to assign each cell to a discrete lineage cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-single-cells))
- **hierarchical-clustering-of-somatic-cna-profiles**: Use when you have per-cell allele-specific integer copy-number calls across single cells and need to define clone structure, especially to cross-validate CNV clones against SNV-genotype clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/hierarchical-clustering-of-somatic-cna-profiles))
